# ​D24.2.46 ESR_EL3, Exception Syndrome Register (EL3)

Source: <https://developer.arm.com/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3->

##### D24.2.46 ESR\_EL3, Exception Syndrome Register (EL3)

The ESR\_EL3 characteristics are:

Purpose
:   Holds syndrome information for an exception taken to EL3.

Configuration
:   This register is present only when EL3 is implemented and FEAT\_AA64 is implemented. Otherwise, direct accesses to ESR\_EL3 are UNDEFINED.

Attributes
:   ESR\_EL3 is a 64-bit register.

###### Field descriptions

![](images/4938-D24.2.46-ESR_EL3-Exception-Syndrome-Register-EL3-img01.svg)

ESR\_EL3 is made UNKNOWN as a result of an exception return from EL3.

When an UNPREDICTABLE instruction is treated as UNDEFINED, and the exception is taken to EL3, the value of ESR\_EL3 is UNKNOWN. The value written to ESR\_EL3 must be consistent with a value that could be created as a result of an exception from the same Exception level that generated the exception as a result of a situation that is not UNPREDICTABLE at that Exception level, in order to avoid the possibility of a privilege violation.

Bits [63:56]
:   Reserved, RES0.

ISS2, bits [55:32]
:   ISS2 encoding for an exception, the bit assignments are:

    ISS2 encoding for an exception from a Data Abort
    :   ![](images/4938-D24.2.46-ESR_EL3-Exception-Syndrome-Register-EL3-img02.svg)

    Bits [23:12]
    :   Reserved, RES0.

    HDBSSF, bit [11]
    :   When FEAT\_HDBSS is implemented, IsSecondStage(Fault), and GetESR\_ELx\_DFSC(EL3) IN {'0011xx', '01001x', '0101xx', '10001x', '1001xx'}:
        :   Indicates that the fault was caused by the HDBSS.

            When DFSC indicates a synchronous External abort on translation table walk or hardware update of translation table, this field indicates whether the fault was caused by a write to the HDBSS.

            This field only applies for stage 2 Permission faults.

            For any other fault, this field is RES0.

            The reset behavior of this field is:

            - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

        Otherwise:
        :   Reserved, RES0.

    TnD, bit [10]
    :   When FEAT\_MTE\_CANONICAL\_TAGS is implemented:
        :   Tag not Data.

            If a memory access generates a Data Abort for a stage 1 Permission fault, this field indicates whether the fault is due to an Allocation Tag access.

    Xs, bits [4:0]
    :   When FEAT\_LS64 is implemented:
        :   When [FEAT\_LS64\_V](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-8-The-Armv8-7-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_ls64_v) is implemented, if a memory access generated by an ST64BV instruction generates a Data Abort exception reported with ISV=1, then this field holds register specifier, Xs.

            When [FEAT\_LS64\_ACCDATA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-8-The-Armv8-7-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_ls64_accdata) is implemented, if a memory access generated by an ST64BV0 instruction generates a Data Abort exception reported with ISV=1, then this field holds register specifier, Xs.

            Otherwise, this field is RES0.

        Otherwise:
        :   Reserved, RES0.

EC, bits [31:26]
:   Exception Class. Indicates the reason for the exception that this register holds information about.

    For each EC value, the table references a subsection that gives information about:

    - The cause of the exception, for example the configuration required to enable the trap.
    - The encoding of the associated ISS.

    Possible values of the EC field are:

   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA32 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b000101
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Trapped MCR or MRC access with (coproc==
     <code>
      0b1110
     </code>
     ).
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_mcr_or_mrc_access" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_mcr_or_mrc_access">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an MCR or MRC access
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA32 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b000110
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Trapped LDC or STC access.
    </p>
    <p>
     The only architected uses of these instruction are:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       An STC to write data to memory from
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-3-Debug-System-registers/-G8-3-17-DBGDTRRXint--Debug-Data-Transfer-Register--Receive?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dbgdtrrxint" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-3-Debug-System-registers/-G8-3-17-DBGDTRRXint--Debug-Data-Transfer-Register--Receive?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dbgdtrrxint">
        DBGDTRRXint
       </a>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       An LDC to read data from memory to
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-3-Debug-System-registers/-G8-3-19-DBGDTRTXint--Debug-Data-Transfer-Register--Transmit?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dbgdtrtxint" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-3-Debug-System-registers/-G8-3-19-DBGDTRTXint--Debug-Data-Transfer-Register--Transmit?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dbgdtrtxint">
        DBGDTRTXint
       </a>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_ldc_or_stc_instruction" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_ldc_or_stc_instruction">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an LDC or STC instruction
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA32 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b000111
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Access to SME, SVE, Advanced SIMD or floating-point functionality trapped by
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-35-CPACR-EL1--Architectural-Feature-Access-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpacr_el1" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-35-CPACR-EL1--Architectural-Feature-Access-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpacr_el1">
      CPACR_EL1
     </a>
     .FPEN,
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2">
      CPTR_EL2
     </a>
     .FPEN,
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2">
      CPTR_EL2
     </a>
     .TFP, or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-38-CPTR-EL3--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL3-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-38-CPTR-EL3--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL3-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el3">
      CPTR_EL3
     </a>
     .TFP control.
    </p>
    <p>
     Excludes exceptions resulting from
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-35-CPACR-EL1--Architectural-Feature-Access-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpacr_el1" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-35-CPACR-EL1--Architectural-Feature-Access-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpacr_el1">
      CPACR_EL1
     </a>
     when the value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2">
      HCR_EL2
     </a>
     .TGE is 1, or because SVE or Advanced SIMD and floating-point are not implemented. These are reported with EC value
     <code>
      0b000000
     </code>
     .
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_access_to_sve__advanced_simd_or_floating_point_functionality__resulting_from_the_fpen_and_tfp_traps" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_access_to_sve__advanced_simd_or_floating_point_functionality__resulting_from_the_fpen_and_tfp_traps">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an access to a register or instruction resulting from the FPEN and TFP traps
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b001001
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Trapped use of a Pointer authentication instruction.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_pointer_authentication_instruction_when_hcr_el2_api__eq__0____scr_el3_api__eq__0" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_pointer_authentication_instruction_when_hcr_el2_api__eq__0____scr_el3_api__eq__0">
      ISS encoding for an exception from a trapped Pointer Authentication instruction
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_PAuth is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b001010
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Trapped execution of any instruction not covered by other EC values.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_any_other_instruction" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_any_other_instruction">
      ISS encoding for an exception from any other instruction
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_LS64 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b001100
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Trapped MRRC access with (coproc==
     <code>
      0b1110
     </code>
     ).
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_mcrr_or_mrrc_access" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_mcrr_or_mrrc_access">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an MCRR or MRRC access
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA32 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b001101
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Branch Target Exception.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_branch_target_identification_instruction" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_branch_target_identification_instruction">
      ISS encoding for a Branch Target Exception
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_BTI is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b001110
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Illegal Execution state.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_illegal_execution_state__or_a_pc_or_sp_alignment_fault" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_illegal_execution_state__or_a_pc_or_sp_alignment_fault">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an Illegal Execution state, or a PC or SP alignment fault
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b010011
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     SMC instruction execution in AArch32 state, when SMC is not disabled.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_smc_instruction_execution_in_aarch32_state" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_smc_instruction_execution_in_aarch32_state">
      ISS encoding for an exception from SMC instruction execution in AArch32 state
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA32 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b010100
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Trapped MSRR, MRRS or System instruction execution in AArch64 state, that is not reported using EC value
     <code>
      0b000000
     </code>
     .
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_msrr__mrrs__or_128_bit_system_instruction_execution_in_aarch64_state" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_msrr__mrrs__or_128_bit_system_instruction_execution_in_aarch64_state">
      ISS encoding for an exception from MSRR, MRRS, or 128-bit System instruction execution in AArch64 state
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_SYSREG128 is implemented or FEAT_SYSINSTR128 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b010101
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     SVC instruction execution in AArch64 state.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_hvc_or_svc_instruction_execution" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_hvc_or_svc_instruction_execution">
      ISS encoding for an exception from HVC or SVC instruction execution
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA64 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b010110
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     HVC instruction execution in AArch64 state, when HVC is not disabled.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_hvc_or_svc_instruction_execution" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_hvc_or_svc_instruction_execution">
      ISS encoding for an exception from HVC or SVC instruction execution
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA64 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b010111
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     SMC instruction execution in AArch64 state, when SMC is not disabled.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_smc_instruction_execution_in_aarch64_state" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_smc_instruction_execution_in_aarch64_state">
      ISS encoding for an exception from SMC instruction execution in AArch64 state
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA64 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b011000
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Trapped MSR, MRS or System instruction execution in AArch64 state, that is not reported using EC values
     <code>
      0b000000
     </code>
     ,
     <code>
      0b000001
     </code>
     or
     <code>
      0b000111
     </code>
     .
    </p>
    <p>
     This includes all instructions that cause exceptions that are part of the encoding space defined in
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-2-System-instruction-class-encoding-overview?lang=en#beijieie" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-2-System-instruction-class-encoding-overview?lang=en#beijieie">
      &lsquo;System instruction class encoding overview&rsquo;
     </a>
     , except for those exceptions reported using EC values
     <code>
      0b000000
     </code>
     ,
     <code>
      0b000001
     </code>
     , or
     <code>
      0b000111
     </code>
     .
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_msr__mrs__or_system_instruction_execution_in_aarch64_state" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_msr__mrs__or_system_instruction_execution_in_aarch64_state">
      ISS encoding for an exception from MSR, MRS, or System instruction execution in AArch64 state
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA64 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b011001
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Access to SVE functionality trapped as a result of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-35-CPACR-EL1--Architectural-Feature-Access-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpacr_el1" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-35-CPACR-EL1--Architectural-Feature-Access-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpacr_el1">
      CPACR_EL1
     </a>
     .ZEN,
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2">
      CPTR_EL2
     </a>
     .ZEN,
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2">
      CPTR_EL2
     </a>
     .TZ, or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-38-CPTR-EL3--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL3-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-38-CPTR-EL3--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL3-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el3">
      CPTR_EL3
     </a>
     .EZ, that is not reported using EC value
     <code>
      0b000000
     </code>
     .
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_access_to_sve_functionality__resulting_from_cpacr_el1_zen__cptr_el2_zen__cptr_el2_tz__or_cptr_el3_ez" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_access_to_sve_functionality__resulting_from_cpacr_el1_zen__cptr_el2_zen__cptr_el2_tz__or_cptr_el3_ez">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an access to SVE functionality, resulting from CPACR_EL1.ZEN, CPTR_EL2.ZEN, CPTR_EL2.TZ, or CPTR_EL3.EZ
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_SVE is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b011100
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Exception from a PAC Fail
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_a_pac_fail_exception" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_a_pac_fail_exception">
      ISS encoding for a PAC Fail exception
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_FPAC is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b011101
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Access to SME functionality trapped as a result of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-35-CPACR-EL1--Architectural-Feature-Access-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpacr_el1" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-35-CPACR-EL1--Architectural-Feature-Access-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpacr_el1">
      CPACR_EL1
     </a>
     .SMEN,
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2">
      CPTR_EL2
     </a>
     .SMEN,
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-37-CPTR-EL2--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el2">
      CPTR_EL2
     </a>
     .TSM,
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-38-CPTR-EL3--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL3-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-38-CPTR-EL3--Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register--EL3-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cptr_el3">
      CPTR_EL3
     </a>
     .ESM, or an attempted execution of an instruction that is illegal because of the value of PSTATE.SM or PSTATE.ZA, that is not reported using EC value
     <code>
      0b000000
     </code>
     .
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_due_to_sme_functionality" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_due_to_sme_functionality">
      ISS encoding for an exception due to SME functionality
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_SME is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b011110
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Granule Protection Check exception
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_granule_protection_check" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_granule_protection_check">
      ISS encoding for a Granule Protection Check exception
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_iss2_an_exception_from_a_granule_protection_check" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_iss2_an_exception_from_a_granule_protection_check">
      ISS2 encoding for a Granule Protection Check exception
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_RME is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b011111
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <small>
      IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED
     </small>
     exception to EL3.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_implementation_defined_exception_to_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_implementation_defined_exception_to_el3">
      ISS encoding for an
      <small>
       IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED
      </small>
      exception to EL3
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b100000
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Instruction Abort from a lower Exception level.
    </p>
    <p>
     Used for MMU faults generated by instruction accesses and synchronous External aborts, including synchronous parity or ECC errors. Not used for debug-related exceptions.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_instruction_abort" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_instruction_abort">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an Instruction Abort
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_iss2_an_exception_from_an_instruction_abort" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_iss2_an_exception_from_an_instruction_abort">
      ISS2 encoding for an exception from an Instruction Abort
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b100001
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Instruction Abort taken without a change in Exception level.
    </p>
    <p>
     Used for MMU faults generated by instruction accesses and synchronous External aborts, including synchronous parity or ECC errors. Not used for debug-related exceptions.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_instruction_abort" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_instruction_abort">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an Instruction Abort
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b100010
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     PC alignment fault exception.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_illegal_execution_state__or_a_pc_or_sp_alignment_fault" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_illegal_execution_state__or_a_pc_or_sp_alignment_fault">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an Illegal Execution state, or a PC or SP alignment fault
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b100100
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Data Abort exception from a lower Exception level.
    </p>
    <p>
     Used for MMU faults generated by data accesses, alignment faults other than those caused by Stack Pointer misalignment, and synchronous External aborts, including synchronous parity or ECC errors. Not used for debug-related exceptions.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_data_abort" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_data_abort">
      ISS encoding for an exception from a Data Abort
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_iss2_an_exception_from_a_data_abort" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_iss2_an_exception_from_a_data_abort">
      ISS2 encoding for an exception from a Data Abort
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b100101
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Data Abort exception taken without a change in Exception level.
    </p>
    <p>
     Used for MMU faults generated by data accesses, alignment faults other than those caused by Stack Pointer misalignment, and synchronous External aborts, including synchronous parity or ECC errors. Not used for debug-related exceptions.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_data_abort" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_data_abort">
      ISS encoding for an exception from a Data Abort
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_iss2_an_exception_from_a_data_abort" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_iss2_an_exception_from_a_data_abort">
      ISS2 encoding for an exception from a Data Abort
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b100110
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     SP alignment fault exception.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_illegal_execution_state__or_a_pc_or_sp_alignment_fault" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_an_illegal_execution_state__or_a_pc_or_sp_alignment_fault">
      ISS encoding for an exception from an Illegal Execution state, or a PC or SP alignment fault
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b100111
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Memory Operation Exception.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_the_memory_copy_and_memory_set_instructions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_the_memory_copy_and_memory_set_instructions">
      ISS encoding for an exception from the Memory Copy and Memory Set instructions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_MOPS is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b101100
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Trapped floating-point exception taken from AArch64 state.
    </p>
    <p>
     This EC value is valid if the implementation supports trapping of floating-point exceptions, otherwise it is reserved. Whether a floating-point implementation supports trapping of floating-point exceptions is
     <small>
      IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED
     </small>
     .
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_trapped_floating_point_exception" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_a_trapped_floating_point_exception">
      ISS encoding for an exception from a trapped floating-point exception
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA64 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b101101
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     GCS exception.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_gcs_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_gcs_exceptions">
      ISS encoding for a GCS exception
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_GCS is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b101111
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     SError exception.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_serror_interrupt" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_serror_interrupt">
      ISS encoding for an SError exception
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b111100
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     BRK instruction execution in AArch64 state.
    </p>
    <p>
     This is reported in
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_esr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_esr_el3">
      ESR_EL3
     </a>
     only if a BRK instruction is executed at EL3. This is the only debug exception that can be taken to EL3 when EL3 is using AArch64.
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_execution_of_a_breakpoint_instruction" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_an_exception_from_execution_of_a_breakpoint_instruction">
      ISS encoding for an exception from execution of a Breakpoint instruction
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_AA64 is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b111101
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Profiling exception
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_a_profiling_exception" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss_a_profiling_exception">
      ISS encoding for a Profiling exception
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-46-ESR-EL3--Exception-Syndrome-Register--EL3-?lang=en#fieldset_aarch64_esr_el3_fieldset_0_iss2_all_other_exceptions">
      ISS2 encoding for all other exceptions
     </a>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     FEAT_EBEP is implemented, or FEAT_SPE_EXC is implemented, or FEAT_TRBE_EXC is implemented
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    All other EC values are reserved by Arm, and:

    - Unused values in the range `0b000000` - `0b101100` (`0x00` - `0x2C`) are reserved for future use for synchronous exceptions.
    - Unused values in the range `0b101101` - `0b111111` (`0x2D` - `0x3F`) are reserved for future use, and might be used for synchronous or asynchronous exceptions.

    The effect of programming this field to a reserved value is that behavior is CONSTRAINED UNPREDICTABLE.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

IL, bit [25]
:   Instruction Length for synchronous exceptions. Possible values of this bit are:

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     IL
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     16-bit instruction trapped.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     32-bit instruction trapped. This value is also used when the exception is one of the following:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       An SError exception.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       An Instruction Abort exception.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       A PC alignment fault exception.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       An SP alignment fault exception.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       A Data Abort exception for which the value of the ISV bit is 0.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       An Illegal Execution state exception.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Any debug exception except for Breakpoint instruction exceptions.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       An exception reported using EC value
       <code>
        0b000000
       </code>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

ISS, bits [24:0]
:   Instruction Specific Syndrome. Architecturally, this field can be defined independently for each defined Exception class. However, in practice, some ISS encodings are used for more than one Exception class.

    Typically, an ISS encoding has a number of subfields. When an ISS subfield holds a register number, the value returned in that field is the AArch64 view of the register number.

    For an exception taken from AArch32 state, see [‘Mapping of the general-purpose registers between the Execution states’](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D1-The-AArch64-System-Level-Programmers--Model/-D1-10-Interprocessing/-D1-10-1-Register-mappings-between-AArch32-state-and-AArch64-state?lang=en#beidfcce).

    If the AArch32 register descriptor is `0b1111`, then:

    - If the instruction that generated the exception was not UNPREDICTABLE, the field takes the value `0b11111`.
    - If the instruction that generated the exception was UNPREDICTABLE, the field takes an UNKNOWN value that must be either:
      - The AArch64 view of the register number of a register that might have been used at the Exception level from which the exception was taken.
      - The value `0b11111`.

    ISS encoding for exceptions with an unknown reason
    :   ![](images/4938-D24.2.46-ESR_EL3-Exception-Syndrome-Register-EL3-img06.svg)

    Bits [24:0]
    :   Reserved, RES0.

        Additional Information for ISS encoding for exceptions with an unknown reason
        :   When an exception is reported using this EC value, the IL field is set to 1.

            This EC value is used for all exceptions that are not covered by any other EC value. This includes exceptions that are generated in the following situations:

            - The attempted execution of an instruction bit pattern that has no allocated instruction or that is not accessible at the current Exception level and Security state, including:
              - A read access using a System register pattern that is not allocated for reads or that does not permit reads at the current Exception level and Security state.
              - A write access using a System register pattern that is not allocated for writes or that does not permit writes at the current Exception level and Security state.
              - Instruction encodings that are unallocated.
              - Instruction encodings for instructions or System registers that are not implemented in the implementation.
            - In Debug state, the attempted execution of an instruction bit pattern that is not accessible in Debug state.
            - In Non-debug state, the attempted execution of an instruction bit pattern that is not accessible in Non-debug state.
            - In AArch32 state, attempted execution of a short vector floating-point instruction.
            - In an implementation that does not include Advanced SIMD and floating-point functionality, an attempted access to Advanced SIMD or floating-point functionality under conditions where that access would be permitted if that functionality was present. This includes the attempted execution of an Advanced SIMD or floating-point instruction, and attempted accesses to Advanced SIMD and floating-point System registers.
            - An exception generated because of the value of one of the [SCTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1).{ITD, SED, CP15BEN} control bits.
            - Attempted execution of:
              - An HVC instruction when disabled by [HCR\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2).HCD or [SCR\_EL3](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3).HCE.
              - An SMC instruction when disabled by [SCR\_EL3](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3).SMD.
              - An HLT instruction when disabled by [EDSCR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-H-External-Debug/-Chapter-H9-External-Debug-Register-Descriptions/-H9-2-External-debug-registers/-H9-2-48-EDSCR--External-Debug-Status-and-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_ext_edscr).HDE.
            - Attempted execution of an MSR or MRS instruction to access [SP\_EL0](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-2-Special-purpose-registers/-C5-2-15-SP-EL0--Stack-Pointer--EL0-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sp_el0) when the value of [SPSel](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-2-Special-purpose-registers/-C5-2-19-SPSel--Stack-Pointer-Select?lang=en#reg_aarch64_spsel).SP is 0.
